\end{table}\par\par\lx@frontmatter@fallback\@@unnumbered@section{subsection}{toc}{\texorpdfstring{$\h 02qq$}{H(0)2qq}}
In reference~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]}, the term from the leading order $qq$ channel is given in equations (41) and (42) as
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(0)}=S_{\perp}\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\delta(1-\xi)\end{equation}This can be expressed as
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(0)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}F^{(0)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\end{equation}where
\begin{equation}F^{(0)}_{d2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{S_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\end{equation}We can translate this into the radial formulation easily using equation~\eqref{eq:maptoradial}, obtaining
\begin{equation}F^{(0)}_{d2qq}(z,\xi,r_{\perp})=\frac{S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\frac{S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})}{2\pi}\biggl(\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\biggr)^{n}r_{\perp}J_{0}(k_{\perp}r_{\perp})\end{equation}which gets integrated as
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(0)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{0}^{\infty}\udc r_{\perp}F^{(0)}_{d2qq}(z,1,r_{\perp})\end{equation}And for the momentum space implementation, equation~\eqref{eq:momentumdipolehardfactorintegral} gives us $\tilde{}\mathcal{H}^{(0)}_{2qq}(\vec{}k_{\perp})=\delta(1-\xi)\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}k_{\perp})$, and then~\eqref{eq:masterformula2momentum} we get
\begin{equation}F^{(0)}_{d2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}k_{\perp})=\frac{1}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\mathcal{F}_{x_{g}}(\vec{}k_{\perp})\end{equation}This case is particularly simple because no momentum integration at all is needed:
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(0)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc zF^{(0)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}k_{\perp})\end{equation}\par In each case $F_{s}$ and $F_{n}$ are, of course, zero.
\par\par\lx@frontmatter@fallback\@@unnumbered@section{subsection}{toc}{\texorpdfstring{$\h 02gg$}{H(0)2gg}}
The expression for the leading-order gluon contribution comes from equations (74) and (75) of~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]}:
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2gg}^{(0)}=S_{\perp}\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}x_{p}g(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/g}(z,\mu)\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})\bigr]^{2}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\delta(1-\xi)\end{equation}The Cartesian position space implementation is straightforward,
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2gg}^{(0)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}F^{(0)}_{d2gg}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\end{equation}where
\begin{equation}F^{(0)}_{d2gg}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{S_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{1}{z^{2}}x_{p}g(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/g}(z,\mu)[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})]^{2}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\end{equation}and again using~\eqref{eq:maptoradial} for the radial implementation, we find
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2gg}^{(0)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\udc r_{\perp}r_{\perp}F^{(0)}_{d2gg}(z,1,r_{\perp})\end{equation}where
\begin{equation}F^{(0)}_{d2gg}(z,\xi,r_{\perp})=\frac{S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}x_{p}g(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/g}(z,\mu)\frac{[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})]^{2}}{2\pi}r_{\perp}J_{0}(k_{\perp}r_{\perp})\end{equation}\par For the momentum space implementation, we get an extra integration, as in~\eqref{eq:momentumdipolesquaredhardfactorintegral}.
We wind up with
\begin{equation}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2gg}^{(0)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\iint\uddc\vec{}q_{\perp}F^{(0)}_{d2gg}(z,1,\vec{}q_{\perp})\end{equation}where
\begin{equation}F^{(0)}_{d2gg}(z,\xi,\vec{}q_{\perp})=\frac{1}{S_{\perp}z^{2}}x_{p}g(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/g}(z,\mu)\mathcal{F}_{x_{g}}(\vec{}q_{\perp})\mathcal{F}_{x_{g}}(\vec{}k_{\perp}-\vec{}q_{\perp})\end{equation}It will be typical for most terms that the momentum-space expressions have to be derived on a case-by-case basis. There are not as many patterns that can be exploited, as there are with the Cartesian expressions.
\par\par\lx@frontmatter@fallback\@@unnumbered@section{subsection}{toc}{\texorpdfstring{$\h 12qq$}{H(1)2qq}}
From equations (41) and (43) of~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]}, the $\mathcal{H}^{(1)}_{2qq}$ contribution to the cross section in the large-$N_{c}$ limit is given as
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(1)}=\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}C_{F}\biggl[\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}+\frac{3}{2}\delta(1-\xi)\biggr]\\
\times\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\biggr)-3C_{F}\delta(1-\xi)e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}k_{\perp}^{2}}\end{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(1)}=\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}C_{F}\biggl[\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}+\frac{3}{2}\delta(1-\xi)\biggr]\\
\times\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\biggr)-3C_{F}\delta(1-\xi)e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}k_{\perp}^{2}}This expression consists of individual terms which follow the pattern of equation~\eqref{eq:unintegrateddipolecontribution}, so we can just substitute into the right side of~\eqref{eq:masterformula2} to get
\begin{multline}F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{1}{4\pi^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{C_{F}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})(1+\xi^{2})\\
\times\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\biggr)\end{multline}F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{1}{4\pi^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{C_{F}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})(1+\xi^{2})\\
\times\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\biggr)and
\begin{multline}F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{3}{4\pi^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{C_{F}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})\\
\times\biggl[\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\biggr)-e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}k_{\perp}^{2}}\biggr]\end{multline}F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{3}{4\pi^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{C_{F}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})\\
\times\biggl[\frac{1}{2}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\biggr)-e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}k_{\perp}^{2}}\biggr]As a delta contribution, this last equation will only ever be evaluated at $\xi=1$, so we can plug that in immediately and get a simpler expression:
\begin{equation}F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{3}{4\pi^{2}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{C_{F}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\ln\frac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\end{equation}Implementing this expression instead of the general one~\eqref{eq:h12qq:cartesian:Fd:unsimplified} reduces the chances of mistakes in the implementation and reduces any numerical errors in the evaluation.
\par Expressions~\eqref{eq:h12qq:cartesian:Fs} and~\eqref{eq:h12qq:cartesian:Fd} go into~\eqref{eq:dipolemasterformula}
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\frac{F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})}{1-\xi}\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\biggl[F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)+F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\biggr]\end{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\frac{F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})}{1-\xi}\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\biggl[F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)+F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\biggr]to produce the final contribution to the cross section.
\par To transform into the radial representation, we use equation~\eqref{eq:maptoradial} to get
\@@amsalign
and the contribution to the cross section will be
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\frac{F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})}{1-\xi}\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\biggl[F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)+F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\biggr]\end{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\frac{F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})}{1-\xi}\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}\biggl[F^{(1)}_{s2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)+F^{(1)}_{d2qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\biggr]\par The logarithmic factors $\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}$ can't be Fourier transformed symbolically, so there is no momentum-space expression for this term.
\par\par\lx@frontmatter@fallback\@@unnumbered@section{subsection}{toc}{\texorpdfstring{$\h 14qq$}{H(1)4qq}}
From equations (41) and (45) of~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]}, this contribution to the cross section is
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{4qq}^{(1)}=-4\pi N_{c}S_{\perp}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\\
\times\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\Biggl\{e^{-i\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}\frac{1}{\xi}\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{s_{\perp}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{}t_{\perp}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\delta(1-\xi)\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\Biggr]\Biggr\}\end{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{4qq}^{(1)}=-4\pi N_{c}S_{\perp}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\\
\times\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\Biggl\{e^{-i\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{(1-\xi)_{+}}\frac{1}{\xi}\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{s_{\perp}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{}t_{\perp}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\delta(1-\xi)\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\Biggr]\Biggr\}where $\vec{}s_{\perp}=\vec{}x_{\perp}-\vec{}b_{\perp}$ and $\vec{}t_{\perp}=\vec{}y_{\perp}-\vec{}b_{\perp}$.
The first term (first and second lines) is easy: it directly translates into a plus-regulated contribution,
\begin{multline}F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})=-\frac{1}{4\pi^{3}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\\
\times e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}/\xi-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{\xi}\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{s_{\perp}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{}t_{\perp}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\end{multline}F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})=-\frac{1}{4\pi^{3}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\\
\times e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}/\xi-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{\xi}\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{s_{\perp}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{}t_{\perp}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}where I've used $\vec{}r_{\perp}+\vec{}s_{\perp}\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}=\vec{}r_{\perp}+\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{\xi}-\vec{}s_{\perp}=\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{\xi}-\vec{}t_{\perp}$ to construct the Fourier exponent.
\par The delta contribution is somewhat more complicated, though, because it has an additional delta function $\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})$ which we need to integrate symbolically first.
We'll have to calculate this integral:
\begin{multline}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\\
\times\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\Biggr]\end{multline}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\\
\times\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\Biggr]Each of the two terms in square brackets is individually divergent, so we need to regulate them, but we can't do the entire integral symbolically because of the unknown gluon distribution $S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}$.
\par The trick is to distribute the $\vec{}t_{\perp}$ integration and impose a lower cutoff $\epsilon$:
\begin{multline}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{r_{\perp}^{\prime}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\Biggr]\end{multline}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{r_{\perp}^{\prime}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\Biggr]Note that integrating with $\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})$ sets $\vec{}r_{\perp}\to\vec{}s_{\perp}$ in the second integral.
Then we change variables $\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\to\xi^{\prime}\vec{}t_{\perp}$.
\begin{multline}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{e^{i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{e^{i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\Biggr]\end{multline}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\Biggl[\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{e^{i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}\frac{e^{i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\Biggr]Now we split the second integral using
\begin{equation}\int_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}=\int_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}-\int_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}^{t_{\perp}<\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\end{equation}The first term of~\eqref{eq:h14qqintegralsplit} combines with the first term of~\eqref{eq:h14qqchangedvariables} to give
\begin{equation}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\bigr]e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\end{equation}and the second term becomes
\begin{multline}-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}^{t_{\perp}<\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\frac{e^{i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
=-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})2\pi\ln\frac{1}{\xi^{\prime}}\end{multline}-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}^{t_{\perp}<\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\frac{e^{i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
=-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})2\pi\ln\frac{1}{\xi^{\prime}}where, in performing the $\vec{}t_{\perp}$ integral, I've used the fact that $t_{\perp}$ is small so the exponential factor can be approximated as $1$.
\par Finally, the integrals over $\xi^{\prime}$ in~\eqref{eq:h14qqfirstfinalterm} and~\eqref{eq:h14qqsecondfinalterm} can be done symbolically, giving
\begin{multline}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\bigr]e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\frac{\mathcal{I}_{1}(\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp})}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})2\pi\biggl(-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\biggr)\end{multline}\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\iint_{t_{\perp}>\epsilon}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\bigr]e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}\frac{\mathcal{I}_{1}(\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp})}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\iint\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})2\pi\biggl(-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\biggr)where
\begin{equation}\mathcal{I}_{1}(x)=-2\gamma_{E}+\frac{i(2-e^{-ix})}{x}+\frac{e^{-ix}-1}{x^{2}}+2\Ci(x)-2i\Si(x)-2\ln x\end{equation}with $\Ci$ and $\Si$ defined as
\@@amsalign
Note that the imaginary parts of $\Ci(x)$ and $\ln x$ for $x<0$ cancel out on the principal branch of each function.
\par So in Cartesian position space, $\mathcal{H}^{(1)}_{4qq}$ actually contributes both a quadrupole term,
\begin{multline}F^{(1)}_{d4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})=\frac{1}{4\pi^{3}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\\
\times\Bigl[S_{Y}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\Bigr]\frac{1}{t_{\perp}^{2}}e^{-i\vec{k}_{\perp}\cdot\vec{r}_{\perp}}\mathcal{I}_{1}(\vec{k}_{\perp}\cdot\vec{t}_{\perp})\end{multline}F^{(1)}_{d4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})=\frac{1}{4\pi^{3}}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\\
\times\Bigl[S_{Y}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{0})\Bigr]\frac{1}{t_{\perp}^{2}}e^{-i\vec{k}_{\perp}\cdot\vec{r}_{\perp}}\mathcal{I}_{1}(\vec{k}_{\perp}\cdot\vec{t}_{\perp})\emph{and} a dipole term
\begin{equation}F^{(1)}_{d2^{\prime}qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}r_{\perp})=\frac{1}{2\pi^{2}}\biggl(-\frac{5}{4}+\frac{\pi^{2}}{3}\biggr)\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}S_{\perp}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)S_{Y}^{(4)}(\vec{}r_{\perp},\vec{0})e^{-i\vec{k}_{\perp}\cdot\vec{r}_{\perp}}\end{equation}and the complete contribution to the cross section is
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{4qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\biggl[\frac{F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})}{1-\xi}\biggr]\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\biggl[F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)+F^{(1)}_{d4qq}(z,1,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\biggr]\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}F^{(1)}_{d2^{\prime}qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\end{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{4qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\biggl[\frac{F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})}{1-\xi}\biggr]\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}\biggl[F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)+F^{(1)}_{d4qq}(z,1,\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})\biggr]\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}F^{(1)}_{d2^{\prime}qq}(z,1,\vec{}r_{\perp})\par To get the momentum space formulation of the plus-regulated part, it will be easiest to start with the Cartesian space result from equations~\eqref{eq:h14qq:cartesian:Fs} as plugged into~\eqref{eq:h14qq:cartesian:xsec}. For simplicity, I'll extract only the parts that depend on $\vec{}s_{\perp}$ and $\vec{}t_{\perp}$:
\begin{equation}\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}/\xi-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\frac{\vec{}s_{\perp}}{s_{\perp}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{}t_{\perp}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}\end{equation}Although it is possible to convert this into momentum space by plugging in the inverse Fourier transform of $S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}$, equation~\eqref{eq:quadrupoleSinvFT}, it's easier to judiciously insert some delta functions.
\begin{equation}\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}/\xi-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\int\uddc\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}s_{\perp}-\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}})\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp}-\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}})\frac{\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{s_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{t_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\end{equation}Note that this expression is exactly equivalent to the previous one. We can then replace the delta functions with their Fourier transforms according to equation~\eqref{eq:ident:deltaFT}
\begin{multline}\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}/\xi-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\\
\times\int\uddc\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-i\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}-\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}})}e^{-i\vec{}q_{2\perp}\cdot(\vec{}t_{\perp}-\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}})}\frac{\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{s_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{t_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\end{multline}\int\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}/\xi-\vec{}t_{\perp})}\\
\times\int\uddc\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}e^{-i\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot(\vec{}s_{\perp}-\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}})}e^{-i\vec{}q_{2\perp}\cdot(\vec{}t_{\perp}-\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}})}\frac{\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{s_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{t_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}which can be rearranged to separate the two groups of position integrals:
\begin{multline}\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i(\vec{}k_{\perp}/\xi+q_{1\perp})\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}e^{i(\vec{}k_{\perp}-\vec{}q_{2\perp})\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\\
\times\int\uddc\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}e^{i\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}e^{i\vec{}q_{2\perp}\cdot\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}\frac{\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{s_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{t_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\end{multline}\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i(\vec{}k_{\perp}/\xi+q_{1\perp})\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}e^{i(\vec{}k_{\perp}-\vec{}q_{2\perp})\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\\
\times\int\uddc\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}\uddc\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}e^{i\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}e^{i\vec{}q_{2\perp}\cdot\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}\frac{\vec{s_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{s_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\cdot\frac{\vec{t_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{t_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}The second line works out to $-(2\pi)^{2}\frac{\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot\vec{}q_{2\perp}}{q_{1\perp}^{2}q_{2\perp}^{2}}$, from equation~\eqref{eq:ident:vecprod}, and the integral over $\vec{}s_{\perp}$ and $\vec{}t_{\perp}$ on the first line is equal to $\frac{1}{S_{\perp}}\mathcal{G}_{x_{g}}\bigl(\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}+\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}k_{\perp}-\vec{}q_{2\perp}\bigr)$ according to equation~\eqref{eq:quadrupoleGdefinition}. Putting this result together with the extra factors that were dropped to get equation~\eqref{eq:h14qqmomentumsingularstart}, we get
\begin{multline}F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})=\frac{1}{\pi}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\\
\times\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{\xi}\mathcal{G}_{x_{g}}\biggl(\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}+\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}k_{\perp}-\vec{}q_{2\perp}\biggr)\frac{\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot\vec{}q_{2\perp}}{q_{1\perp}^{2}q_{2\perp}^{2}}\end{multline}F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})=\frac{1}{\pi}\frac{\alpha_{s}}{2\pi}\frac{N_{c}}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\\
\times\frac{1+\xi^{2}}{\xi}\mathcal{G}_{x_{g}}\biggl(\frac{\vec{}k_{\perp}}{\xi}+\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}k_{\perp}-\vec{}q_{2\perp}\biggr)\frac{\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot\vec{}q_{2\perp}}{q_{1\perp}^{2}q_{2\perp}^{2}}which will be integrated over $\vec{}q_{1\perp}$ and $\vec{}q_{2\perp}$ in equation~\eqref{eq:h14qq:momentum:xsec}.
\par For the delta function term, we can use the identity in equation (34) of~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]}, with $\bar{\vec{r}}_{\perp}=\vec{}t_{\perp}$. Multiplying both sides of that equation by $-\frac{1}{4\pi}e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}$ and taking the complex conjugate gives
\begin{multline}-\frac{1}{4\pi}\iint\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}e^{i(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\vec{}k_{\perp})\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\\
=\biggl[\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\iint\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}\biggr]\end{multline}-\frac{1}{4\pi}\iint\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}e^{i(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\vec{}k_{\perp})\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\\
=\biggl[\frac{e^{-i(1-\xi^{\prime})\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}{t_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\iint\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}\biggr]Since this is going to be integrated over $\vec{}t_{\perp}$, the $e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}$ factor in the second term will be equal to 1 anyway (because of the delta function), and taking that into account, the right side of this equation is exactly the last factor of the delta-function term in equation~\eqref{eq:xsec14qq}. Restoring the factors and the integral from the second line of~\eqref{eq:xsec14qq} gives
\begin{equation}-\frac{1}{4\pi}\iint\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\underbrace{\iint\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(4)}(\vec{}s_{\perp},\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}s_{\perp}}e^{i\vec{}q_{1\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}}_{\mathcal{G}_{x_{g}}(\vec{}k_{\perp},-\vec{}q_{1\perp})/S_{\perp}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\end{equation}Restoring the factors from the first line of~\eqref{eq:xsec14qq} gives the delta contribution. The plus prescription factor, $\frac{1}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}$, can be handled symbolically:
\begin{equation}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{(1-\xi^{\prime})_{+}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}=\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{1-\xi^{\prime}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}-\frac{2}{1-\xi^{\prime}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\end{equation}making the final expression for the delta contribution
\begin{multline}F^{(1)}_{d4qq}=-N_{c}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\mathcal{G}_{x_{g}}(\vec{}k_{\perp},-\vec{}q_{1\perp})\\
\times\biggl[\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{1-\xi^{\prime}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}-\frac{2}{1-\xi^{\prime}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\biggr]\end{multline}F^{(1)}_{d4qq}=-N_{c}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\sum_{q}x_{p}q(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/q}(z,\mu)\mathcal{G}_{x_{g}}(\vec{}k_{\perp},-\vec{}q_{1\perp})\\
\times\biggl[\frac{1+\xi^{\prime 2}}{1-\xi^{\prime}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}-\frac{2}{1-\xi^{\prime}}\ln\frac{(\vec{}q_{1\perp}-\vec{}k_{\perp})^{2}}{k_{\perp}^{2}}\biggr]The complete contribution from $\mathcal{H}^{(1)}_{4qq}$ in momentum space is then
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{4qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}\biggl[\frac{F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})}{1-\xi}\biggr]\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}F^{(1)}_{d4qq}(z,1,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\xi^{\prime})\end{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{4qq}^{(1)}=\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int_{\tau/z}^{1}\udc\xi\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}\biggl[\frac{F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,\xi,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})-F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})}{1-\xi}\biggr]\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\uddc\vec{}q_{2\perp}F^{(1)}_{s4qq}(z,1,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\vec{}q_{2\perp})\ln\biggl(1-\frac{\tau}{z}\biggr)\\
+\int_{\tau}^{1}\udc z\int\uddc\vec{}q_{1\perp}\int_{0}^{1}\udc\xi^{\prime}F^{(1)}_{d4qq}(z,1,\vec{}q_{1\perp},\xi^{\prime})with the functions $F^{(1)}_{s4qq}$ and $F^{(1)}_{d4qq}$ from equations~\eqref{eq:h14qq:momentum:Fs} and~\eqref{eq:h14qq:momentum:Fd} respectively.
\par\par\lx@frontmatter@fallback\@@unnumbered@section{subsection}{toc}{\texorpdfstring{$\h 12gg$}{H(1)2gg}}
From equations (74) and (76) in~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]}, the contribution of the $\mathcal{H}^{(1)}_{2gg}$ term to the cross section is
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{2gg}^{(1)}=N_{c}S_{\perp}\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}x_{p}g(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/g}(z,\mu)\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})\bigr]^{2}\\
\times\Biggl[\biggl\{2\biggl[\frac{\xi}{(1-\xi)_{+}}+\frac{1-\xi}{\xi}+\xi(1-\xi)\biggr]+\biggl(\frac{11}{6}-\frac{2N_{f}T_{R}}{3N_{c}}\biggr)\delta(1-\xi)\biggr\}\ln\frac{c_{0}^{2}}{r_{\perp}^{2}\mu^{2}}\biggl(e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}/\xi}\biggr)\\

\par To put $F^{(1)}_{d2q\bar{q}}$ in a a form suitable for numerical integration, we'll have to regularize the terms in brackets. The procedure is basically similar to the one used with $\mathcal{H}^{(1)}_{4qq}$, with some slight differences because the formula is different. We start by extracting the position-dependent factors,
\begin{equation}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}s_{\perp})S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\biggl[\frac{e^{-i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}}{r_{\perp}^{2}}-\delta^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})\int\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\frac{e^{i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}}}{r_{\perp}^{\prime 2}}\biggr]\end{equation}After changing variables $\vec{}s_{\perp}\to\vec{}r_{\perp}$ (with Jacobian $1$) and excising a region of radius $\epsilon$ around the poles in $\vec{}r_{\perp}$ and $\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}$, then changing variables $\vec{}r_{\perp}^{\prime}\to-\xi^{\prime}\vec{}r_{\perp}$ in the second term, we get
\begin{multline}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon}\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp}-\vec{}r_{\perp})\frac{e^{-i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}}{r_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\bigr]^{2}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{e^{-i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}}{r_{\perp}^{2}}\end{multline}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon}\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp}-\vec{}r_{\perp})\frac{e^{-i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}}{r_{\perp}^{2}}\\
-\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\bigr]^{2}e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\frac{e^{-i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}}{r_{\perp}^{2}}We split the second integral into two pieces, $\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}=\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon}-\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon}^{r_{\perp}<\epsilon/\xi^{\prime}}$, so the total integral turns into
\begin{multline}\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})e^{-i\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}t_{\perp}}\int_{r_{\perp}>\epsilon}\frac{\uddc\vec{}r_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{2}}\bigl[S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp}-\vec{}r_{\perp})-S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})\bigr]\frac{e^{-i\xi^{\prime}\vec{}k_{\perp}\cdot\vec{}r_{\perp}}}{r_{\perp}^{2}}\\

From equations (74) and (78) of~\cite[cite]{[\@@bibref{}{Chirilli:2012jd}{}{}]} we get this term to be
\begin{multline}\biggl(\frac{\udddc\sigma}{\udc Y\uddc\vec{p}_{\perp}}\biggr)_{6gg}^{(1)}=-S_{\perp}\int\frac{\udc z\udc\xi}{z^{2}}x_{p}g(x_{p},\mu)D_{\mathit{{h}{}_{\mspace{-3mu}\scriptstyle{}}^{\mspace{-1mu}\scriptstyle{}}}/g}(x_{p},\mu)\int\frac{\uddc\vec{}s_{\perp}\uddc\vec{}t_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{4}}S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}s_{\perp})S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}t_{\perp})S_{x_{g}}^{(2)}(\vec{}r_{\perp})\frac{\alpha_{\text{s}}}{2\pi}\\
